It's fundamental to information retrieval and search engines.\n\n## Core Concepts\n\n### 1. Term Frequency (TF)\nMeasures how frequently a term appears in a document.\n\n**Formula:**\n```\nTF(t, d) = count(t in d) / total_terms(d)\n```\n\n**Example:**\n```python\ndef compute_term_frequency(tokens):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute normalized term frequencies.\n\n    Args:\n        tokens: List of tokens from a document\n\n    Returns:\n        Dict mapping term to frequency\n    \"\"\"\n    if not tokens:\n        return {}\n\n    from collections import defaultdict\n\n    # Count occurrences\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for token in tokens:\n        counts[token] += 1\n\n    # Normalize by total count\n    total = len(tokens)\n    return {term: count / total for term, count in counts.items()}\n\n# Example\ntokens = [\"machine\", \"learning\", \"machine\", \"algorithm\"]\ntf = compute_term_frequency(tokens)\n# Result: {\"machine\": 0.5, \"learning\": 0.25, \"algorithm\": 0.25}\n```\n\n### 2. Document Frequency (DF)\nCounts how many documents contain a term.\n\n```python\ndef compute_document_frequencies(documents_terms):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute document frequency for each term.\n\n    Args:\n        documents_terms: Dict of {doc_id: set of terms}\n\n    Returns:\n        Dict mapping term to document frequency\n    \"\"\"\n    df = {}\n    all_terms = set()\n\n    # Collect all terms\n    for terms in documents_terms.values():\n        all_terms.update(terms)\n\n    # Count documents containing each term\n    for term in all_terms:\n        count = sum(1 for doc_terms in documents_terms.values()\n                    if term in doc_terms)\n        df[term] = count\n\n    return df\n```\n\n### 3. Inverse Document Frequency (IDF)\nMeasures how rare or common a term is across all documents.\n\n**Formula (smoothed):**\n```\nIDF(t) = log(N / DF(t)) + 1\n```\n\nWhere:\n- N = total number of documents\n- DF(t) = number of documents containing term t\n- +1 is smoothing to avoid division by zero\n\n**Properties:**\n- Common terms (high DF) → low IDF → less important\n- Rare terms (low DF) → high IDF → more important\n\n```python\nimport math\n\ndef compute_idf(document_frequencies, num_documents):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute IDF scores for all terms.\n\n    Args:\n        document_frequencies: Dict of term -> document frequency\n        num_documents: Total number of documents\n\n    Returns:\n        Dict of term -> IDF score\n    \"\"\"\n    idf = {}\n    for term, df in document_frequencies.items():\n        idf[term] = math.log(num_documents / df) + 1\n    return idf\n\n# Example\ndf = {\"machine\": 100, \"learning\": 50, \"rare\": 2}\nnum_docs = 1000\nidf = compute_idf(df, num_docs)\n# Result: {\"machine\": 3.3, \"learning\": 4.0, \"rare\": 7.2}\n```\n\n### 4. TF-IDF Score\nCombines TF and IDF to measure term importance.\n\n**Formula:**\n```\nTF-IDF(t, d) = TF(t, d) × IDF(t)\n```\n\n```python\ndef compute_tfidf(tf, idf):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute TF-IDF scores for a document.\n\n    Args:\n        tf: Dict of term -> term frequency\n        idf: Dict of term -> IDF score\n\n    Returns:\n        Dict of term -> TF-IDF score\n    \"\"\"\n    tfidf = {}\n    for term, freq in tf.items():\n        if term in idf:\n            tfidf[term] = freq * idf[term]\n    return tfidf\n```\n\n## Inverted Index\n\n### Structure\nMaps each term to a list of documents containing it.\n\n```python\ndef build_inverted_index(documents_tfidf):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build inverted index from TF-IDF scores.\n\n    Args:\n        documents_tfidf: Dict of {doc_id: {term: tfidf_score}}\n\n    Returns:\n        Dict of {term: [(doc_id, tfidf_score), ...]}\n    \"\"\"\n    inverted_index = {}\n\n    for doc_id, tfidf in documents_tfidf.items():\n        for term, score in tfidf.items():\n            if term not in inverted_index:\n                inverted_index[term] = []\n            inverted_index[term].append((doc_id, score))\n\n    # Sort posting lists by score (descending)\n    for term in inverted_index:\n        inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    return inverted_index\n```\n\n### Posting List\nList of (document_id, score) pairs for a term.\n\n```\n\"machine\" -> [(doc_5, 0.8), (doc_2, 0.6), (doc_9, 0.4), ...]\n```\n\n## Cosine Similarity Search\n\n### Vector Representation\nEach document is a vector in term space.\n\n```\ndoc_vector = {term1: tfidf1, term2: tfidf2, ...}\n```\n\n### Cosine Similarity Formula\n```\ncosine_sim(q, d) = (q · d) / (||q|| × ||d||)\n```\n\nWhere:\n- q · d = dot product of query and document vectors\n- ||q|| = L2 norm of query vector\n- ||d|| = L2 norm of document vector\n\n### Implementation\n```python\nimport math\n\ndef compute_vector_norm(vector):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute L2 norm of a vector.\n\n    Args:\n        vector: Dict of term -> value\n\n    Returns:\n        L2 norm (float)\n    \"\"\"\n    norm_squared = sum(value * value for value in vector.values())\n    return math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\ndef cosine_similarity(query_vector, doc_vector, query_norm, doc_norm):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute cosine similarity between query and document.\n\n    Args:\n        query_vector: Dict of term -> TF-IDF\n        doc_vector: Dict of term -> TF-IDF\n        query_norm: Pre-computed L2 norm of query\n        doc_norm: Pre-computed L2 norm of document\n\n    Returns:\n        Cosine similarity score (0 to 1)\n    \"\"\"\n    if doc_norm == 0 or query_norm == 0:\n        return 0.0\n\n    # Dot product (only non-zero for common terms)\n    dot_product = sum(\n        query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n        for term in query_vector\n    )\n\n    # Cosine similarity\n    return dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n```\n\n## Search Algorithm\n\n### Steps\n1. Tokenize query\n2. Compute query TF-IDF vector\n3. Find candidate documents (containing query terms)\n4. Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n5. Return top-k results\n\n```python\ndef search(query_text, index, top_k=10):\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for documents matching query.\n\n    Args:\n        query_text: Search query string\n        index: TF-IDF index structure\n        top_k: Number of results to return\n\n    Returns:\n        List of (doc_id, score) tuples\n    \"\"\"\n    from heapq import nlargest\n\n    # Step 1: Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query_text)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Step 2: Compute query TF-IDF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequency(query_tokens)\n    query_vector = {}\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            query_vector[term] = tf * index.idf[term]\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = compute_vector_norm(query_vector)\n\n    # Step 3: Find candidates using inverted index\n    candidates = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidates.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Step 4: Compute similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidates:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors[doc_id]\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms[doc_id]\n\n        similarity = cosine_similarity(\n            query_vector, doc_vector, query_norm, doc_norm\n        )\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Step 5: Get top-k\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    return top_results\n```\n\n## Optimization Techniques\n\n### 1. Pre-compute Document Norms\nStore L2 norms during indexing to avoid recomputation.\n\n```python\n# During indexing\ndoc_norms = {}\nfor doc_id, vector in doc_vectors.items():\n    doc_norms[doc_id] = compute_vector_norm(vector)\n```\n\n### 2. Alternative IDF Formula\nWithout smoothing:\n```\nIDF(t) = log(N / DF(t))\n```\n\nWith smoothing (prevents log(0)):\n```\nIDF(t) = log((N + 1) / (DF(t) + 1)) + 1\n```\n\n### 3. BM25 (Best Match 25)\nMore sophisticated ranking function:\n```\nBM25(t, d) = IDF(t) × (TF(t, d) × (k1 + 1)) / (TF(t, d) + k1 × (1 - b + b × |d| / avgdl))\n```\nWhere k1 and b are tuning parameters.\n\n## Text Preprocessing\n\n### Tokenization\n```python\nimport re\n\nTOKEN_PATTERN = re.compile(r'\\b[a-zA-Z]{2,}\\b')\n\ndef tokenize(text):\n    \"\"\"Extract words from text\"\"\"\n    # Find all words (2+ letters)\n    tokens = TOKEN_PATTERN.findall(text.lower())\n    return tokens\n```\n\n### Stop Word Removal\n```python\nSTOP_WORDS = frozenset(['the', 'a', 'an', 'and', 'or', ...])\n\ndef remove_stop_words(tokens):\n    \"\"\"Filter out common stop words\"\"\"\n    return [t for t in tokens if t not in STOP_WORDS]\n```\n\n### Stemming/Lemmatization\nReduce words to root form (requires external library like NLTK).\n\n```python\n# Example with simple suffix removal\ndef simple_stem(word):\n    \"\"\"Basic stemming (remove common suffixes)\"\"\"\n    suffixes = ['ing', 'ed', 's', 'es']\n    for suffix in suffixes:\n        if word.endswith(suffix) and len(word) > len(suffix) + 2:\n            return word[:-len(suffix)]\n    return word\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n1. **Normalize text**: Lowercase, remove punctuation\n2. **Remove stop words**: Filter common words with no semantic value\n3. **Pre-compute IDF**: Calculate once during indexing\n4. **Store document norms**: Avoid recomputing during search\n5. **Use sparse vectors**: Only store non-zero values\n6. **Sort posting lists**: By score for efficient top-k retrieval\n7. **Validate inputs**: Handle empty documents and queries\n\n## Common Pitfalls\n\n1. **Division by zero**: Use smoothing in IDF calculation\n2. **Memory explosion**: Use sparse representations\n3. **Slow search**: Pre-compute and cache expensive operations\n4. **Poor tokenization**: Results in noisy or missing features\n5. **Not filtering stop words**: Inflates index size unnecessarily\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Time Measurement\n```python\nimport time\n\n# Basic timing\nstart = time.perf_counter()\n# ... code to measure ...\nelapsed = time.perf_counter() - start\nprint(f\"Elapsed: {elapsed:.3f} seconds\")\n\n# Context manager for timing\nclass Timer:\n    def __enter__(self):\n        self.start = time.perf_counter()\n        return self\n\n    def __exit__(self, *args):\n        self.elapsed = time.perf_counter() - self.start\n        print(f\"Elapsed: {self.elapsed:.3f}s\")\n\n# Usage\nwith Timer():\n    # code to measure\n    pass\n```\n\n### 2. Use Appropriate Data Structures\n```python\n# SLOW: List membership testing\nslow_list = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, ...]\nif x in slow_list:  # O(n)\n    pass\n\n# FAST: Set membership testing\nfast_set = {1, 2, 3, 4, 5, ...}\nif x in fast_set:  # O(1)\n    pass\n\n# Dictionary for counting\nfrom collections import defaultdict\n\n# SLOW: Manual counting\ncounts = {}\nfor item in items:\n    if item in counts:\n        counts[item] += 1\n    else:\n        counts[item] = 1\n\n# FAST: defaultdict\ncounts = defaultdict(int)\nfor item in items:\n    counts[item] += 1\n\n# FASTEST: Counter\nfrom collections import Counter\ncounts = Counter(items)\n```\n\n### 2. List Comprehensions vs Loops\n```python\n# SLOW: Loop with append\nresult = []\nfor i in range(1000):\n    result.append(i * 2)\n\n# FAST: List comprehension\nresult = [i * 2 for i in range(1000)]\n\n# FASTEST for large data: Generator (memory efficient)\nresult = (i * 2 for i in range(1000000))\n```\n\n### 3. Avoid Repeated Computation\n```python\n# SLOW: Repeated computation\ndef slow_search(data, query):\n    results = []\n    for item in data:\n        if expensive_function(item) == query:  # Called for each item\n            results.append(item)\n    return results\n\n# FAST: Pre-compute\ndef fast_search(data, query):\n    # Pre-compute expensive function once\n    computed = {item: expensive_function(item) for item in data}\n    results = [item for item, value in computed.items() if value == query]\n    return results\n\n# BETTER: Cache if used multiple times\nfrom functools import lru_cache\n\n@lru_cache(maxsize=1000)\ndef expensive_function(item):\n    # Expensive computation\n    return result\n```\n\n### 2. Use Built-in Functions\nBuilt-in functions are implemented in C and are much faster.\n\n```python\n# SLOW: Manual sum\ntotal = 0\nfor x in numbers:\n    total += x\n\n# FAST: Built-in sum\ntotal = sum(numbers)\n\n# SLOW: Manual max\nmax_val = numbers[0]\nfor x in numbers:\n    if x > max_val:\n        max_val = x\n\n# FAST: Built-in max\nmax_val = max(numbers)\n```\n\n### 3. Use NumPy for Numerical Operations\n```python\nimport numpy as np\n\n# SLOW: Python list operations\ndata = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]\nresult = [x * 2 + 1 for x in data]\n\n# FAST: NumPy vectorized operations\ndata = np.array([1, 2, 3, 4, 5])\nresult = data * 2 + 1  # Vectorized, much faster\n```\n\n## Loop Optimization\n\n### 1. Move Invariants Outside Loops\n```python\n# SLOW: Computation inside loop\nfor item in items:\n    constant_value = expensive_computation()  # Same each iteration!\n    result = item * constant_value\n\n# FAST: Move outside loop\nconstant_value = expensive_computation()  # Compute once\nfor item in items:\n    result = item * constant_value\n```\n\n### 2. Avoid Attribute Lookup in Loops\n```python\n# SLOW: Repeated attribute lookup\nfor i in range(len(items)):\n    items.append(i)  # Lookup 'append' each time\n\n# FAST: Local reference\nappend = items.append\nfor i in range(len(items)):\n    append(i)\n```\n\n### 3. Use enumerate() Instead of range(len())\n```python\n# SLOW: Index-based iteration\nfor i in range(len(items)):\n    item = items[i]\n    process(item, i)\n\n# FAST: enumerate\nfor i, item in enumerate(items):\n    process(item, i)\n```\n\n## Memory Optimization\n\n### 1. Generators for Large Sequences\n```python\n# Memory-heavy: Creates entire list in memory\ndef get_squares(n):\n    return [i * i for i in range(n)]\n\n# Memory-efficient: Generates values on-demand\ndef get_squares(n):\n    return (i * i for i in range(n))\n\n# Or use yield\ndef get_squares(n):\n    for i in range(n):\n        yield i * i\n```\n\n### 2. Use Slots for Classes\n```python\n# Default: Each instance has __dict__\nclass Regular:\n    def __init__(self, x, y):\n        self.x = x\n        self.y = y\n\n# Optimized: __slots__ reduces memory\nclass Optimized:\n    __slots__ = ['x', 'y']\n\n    def __init__(self, x, y):\n        self.x = x\n        self.y = y\n\n# Memory savings significant for many instances\n```\n\n### 3. Delete Unused Large Objects\n```python\nlarge_data = process_data()\nresult = extract_result(large_data)\ndel large_data  # Free memory immediately\n```\n\n## Parallel Processing\n\n### 1. Multiprocessing for CPU-Bound Tasks\n```python\nfrom multiprocessing import Pool, cpu_count\n\ndef process_item(item):\n    # CPU-intensive work\n    return result\n\n# Sequential\nresults = [process_item(item) for item in items]\n\n# Parallel\nwith Pool(processes=cpu_count()) as pool:\n    results = pool.map(process_item, items)\n```\n\n### 2. Threading for I/O-Bound Tasks\n```python\nfrom concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor\n\ndef fetch_url(url):\n    # I/O-bound work (network request)\n    return data\n\n# Parallel I/O\nwith ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=10) as executor:\n    results = list(executor.map(fetch_url, urls))\n```\n\n## Dictionary and Set Optimization\n\n### 1. Pre-size Dictionaries\n```python\n# May require resizing during growth\ndata = {}\nfor i in range(1000000):\n    data[i] = i\n\n# Pre-allocated (Python 3.9+)\ndata = dict.fromkeys(range(1000000))\nfor i in range(1000000):\n    data[i] = i\n```\n\n### 2. Use get() with Default\n```python\n# SLOW: Check then access\nif key in dictionary:\n    value = dictionary[key]\nelse:\n    value = default\n\n# FAST: get() with default\nvalue = dictionary.get(key, default)\n\n# For updating counts\ncounts[key] = counts.get(key, 0) + 1\n```\n\n## Function Call Optimization\n\n### 1. Reduce Function Calls\n```python\n# SLOW: Many function calls\ndef process(x):\n    return helper(x)\n\nresult = [process(x) for x in data]\n\n# FAST: Inline or reduce calls\nresult = [helper(x) for x in data]\n```\n\n### 2. Use Local Variables for Globals\n```python\nimport math\n\n# SLOW: Global lookup in loop\ndef compute(values):\n    return [math.sqrt(x) for x in values]\n\n# FAST: Local reference\ndef compute(values):\n    sqrt = math.sqrt  # Local reference\n    return [sqrt(x) for x in values]\n```\n\n## Common Patterns\n\n### 1. Batch Processing\n```python\ndef process_in_batches(items, batch_size=1000):\n    \"\"\"Process items in batches to reduce overhead\"\"\"\n    for i in range(0, len(items), batch_size):\n        batch = items[i:i + batch_size]\n        yield process_batch(batch)\n```\n\n### 2. Early Exit\n```python\n# SLOW: Process all even if result found early\ndef find_item(items, target):\n    results = [item for item in items if matches(item, target)]\n    if results:\n        return results[0]\n    return None\n\n# FAST: Early exit\ndef find_item(items, target):\n    for item in items:\n        if matches(item, target):\n            return item\n    return None\n```\n\n### 3. Avoid Copying\n```python\n# SLOW: Creates copy\ndef process(data):\n    data_copy = data.copy()\n    # Process data_copy\n    return data_copy\n\n# FAST: Modify in-place if possible\ndef process(data):\n    # Modify data directly\n    return data\n```\n\n## Measurement Best Practices\n\n### 1. Use Representative Data\nTest with realistic data size and distribution.\n\n### 2. Warm-up Runs\n```python\n# First run may be slower due to caching\ndef benchmark(func, data, runs=10):\n    # Warm-up\n    func(data)\n\n    # Measure\n    times = []\n    for _ in range(runs):\n        start = time.perf_counter()\n        func(data)\n        times.append(time.perf_counter() - start)\n\n    return sum(times) / len(times)\n```\n\n### 3. Compare Fairly\n```python\ndef compare_implementations(impl1, impl2, data):\n    \"\"\"Compare two implementations\"\"\"\n    time1 = benchmark(impl1, data)\n    time2 = benchmark(impl2, data)\n\n    speedup = time1 / time2\n    print(f\"Implementation 1: {time1:.3f}s\")\n    print(f\"Implementation 2: {time2:.3f}s\")\n    print(f\"Speedup: {speedup:.2f}x\")\n```\n\n## Anti-Patterns to Avoid\n\n1. **Premature optimization**: Profile first, optimize bottlenecks\n2. **Micro-optimizations**: Focus on algorithmic improvements\n3. **Sacrificing readability**: Keep code maintainable\n4. **Not measuring**: Always measure impact of optimizations\n5. **Optimizing cold paths**: Focus on hot code paths (frequently executed)\n\n## Quick Wins Checklist\n\n- [ ] Use appropriate data structures (set for membership, dict for lookup)\n- [ ] Use list comprehensions instead of append loops\n- [ ] Pre-compute values used repeatedly\n- [ ] Use built-in functions (sum, max, min, etc.)\n- [ ] Move invariants outside loops\n- [ ] Use generators for large sequences\n- [ ] Cache expensive function results\n- [ ] Use multiprocessing for CPU-bound parallel work\n- [ ] Profile before optimizing\n- [ ] Test that optimizations actually improve performance\n\n## Tools and Libraries\n\n### Performance\n- `time.perf_counter()` - High-resolution timing\n- `timeit` - Benchmark small code snippets\n- `cProfile` - Function-level profiling\n- `line_profiler` - Line-by-line profiling\n- `memory_profiler` - Memory usage profiling\n\n### Optimization\n- `numpy` - Pre-size Dictionaries\n```python\n# May require resizing during growth\ndata = {}\nfor i in range(1000000):\n    data[i] = i\n\n# Pre-allocated (Python 3.9+)\ndata = dict.fromkeys(range(1000000))\nfor i in range(1000000):\n    data[i] = i\n```\n\n### 2. Use get() with Default\n```python\n# SLOW: Check then access\nif key in dictionary:\n    value = dictionary[key]\nelse:\n    value = default\n\n# FAST: get() with default\nvalue = dictionary.get(key, default)\n\n# For updating counts\ncounts[key] = counts.get(key, 0) + 1\n```\n\n## Function Call Optimization\n\n### 1. Reduce Function Calls\n```python\n# SLOW: Many function calls\ndef process(x):\n    return helper(x)\n\nresult = [process(x) for x in data]\n\n# FAST: Inline or reduce calls\nresult = [helper(x) for x in data]\n```\n\n### 2. Use Local Variables for Globals\n```python\nimport math\n\n# SLOW: Global lookup in loop\ndef compute(values):\n    return [math.sqrt(x) for x in values]\n\n# FAST: Local reference\ndef compute(values):\n    sqrt = math.sqrt  # Local reference\n    return [sqrt(x) for x in values]\n```\n\n## Common Patterns\n\n### 1. Batch Processing\n```python\ndef process_in_batches(items, batch_size=1000):\n    \"\"\"Process items in batches to reduce overhead\"\"\"\n    for i in range(0, len(items), batch_size):\n        batch = items[i:i + batch_size]\n        yield process_batch(batch)\n```\n\n### 2. Early Exit\n```python\n# SLOW: Process all even if result found early\ndef find_item(items, target):\n    results = [item for item in items if matches(item, target)]\n    if results:\n        return results[0]\n    return None\n\n# FAST: Early exit\ndef find_item(items, target):\n    for item in items:\n        if matches(item, target):\n            return item\n    return None\n```\n\n### 3. Avoid Copying\n```python\n# SLOW: Creates copy\ndef process(data):\n    data_copy = data.copy()\n    # Process data_copy\n    return data_copy\n\n# FAST: Modify in-place if possible\ndef process(data):\n    # Modify data directly\n    return data\n```\n\n## Measurement Best Practices\n\n### 1. Use Representative Data\nTest with realistic data size and distribution.\n\n### 2. Warm-up Runs\n```python\n# First run may be slower due to caching\ndef benchmark(func, data, runs=10):\n    # Warm-up\n    func(data)\n\n    # Measure\n    times = []\n    for _ in range(runs):\n        start = time.perf_counter()\n        func(data)\n        times.append(time.perf_counter() - start)\n\n    return sum(times) / len(times)\n```\n\n### 3. Compare Fairly\n```python\ndef compare_implementations(impl1, impl2, data):\n    \"\"\"Compare two implementations\"\"\"\n    time1 = benchmark(impl1, data)\n    time2 = benchmark(impl2, data)\n\n    speedup = time1 / time2\n    print(f\"Implementation 1: {time1:.3f}s\")\n    print(f\"Implementation 2: {time2:.3f}s\")\n    print(f\"Speedup: {speedup:.2f}x\")\n```\n\n## Anti-Patterns to Avoid\n\n1. **Premature optimization**: Profile first, optimize bottlenecks\n2. **Micro-optimizations**: Focus on algorithmic improvements\n3. **Sacrificing readability**: Keep code maintainable\n4. **Not measuring**: Always measure impact of optimizations\n5. **Optimizing cold paths**: Focus on hot code paths (frequently executed)\n\n## Quick Wins Checklist\n\n- [ ] Use appropriate data structures (set for membership, dict for lookup)\n- [ ] Use list comprehensions instead of append loops\n- [ ] Pre-compute values used repeatedly\n- [ ] Use built-in functions (sum, max, min, etc.)\n- [ ] Move invariants outside loops\n- [ ] Use generators for large sequences\n- [ ] Cache expensive function results\n- [ ] Use multiprocessing for CPU-bound parallel work\n- [ ] Profile before optimizing\n- [ ] Test that optimizations actually improve performance\n\n## Tools and Libraries\n\n### Performance\n- `time.perf_counter()` - High-resolution timing\n- `timeit` - Benchmark small code snippets\n- `cProfile` - Function-level profiling\n- `line_profiler` - Line-by-line profiling\n- `memory_profiler` - Memory usage profiling\n\n### Optimization\n- `numpy` - Fast numerical operations\n- `numba` - JIT compilation for numeric code\n- `multiprocessing` - Parallel processing\n- `functools.lru_cache` - Function result caching\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

documents.\n\n    Args:\n        chunk_data: Tuple of (documents_chunk,)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    documents = chunk_data\n\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Steps:\n    1. Split documents into chunks\n    2. Process chunks in parallel (tokenize, compute TF)\n    3. Merge results from all chunks\n    4. Calculate document frequencies (sequential)\n    5. Compute IDF scores (sequential)\n    6. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores (parallel)\n    7. Compute document vector norms (parallel)\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Size of each chunk for processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # Determine number of workers\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Create chunks\n    chunks = _create_chunks(documents, chunk_size)\n\n    # Step 2: Process chunks in parallel (tokenize and compute TF)\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        chunk_results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks)\n\n    # Step 3: Merge results from all chunks\n    doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary = _merge_chunk_results(chunk_results)\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n    # Step 4: Calculate document frequencies (sequential - fast operation)\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 5: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast operation)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Sort by score descending\n            posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n            partial_index[term] = posting_list\n        return partial_index\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        partial_indexes = pool.map(_build_inverted_index_chunk, vocab_chunks)\n\n    # Merge inverted indexes\n    for partial_index in partial_indexes:\n        index.inverted_index.update(partial_index)\n\n    # Step 7: Build document vectors and compute norms (parallel)\n    doc_id_chunks = _create_chunks(list(doc_term_freqs.keys()), chunk_size=1000)\n\n    def _build_doc_vectors_chunk(doc_ids):\n        \"\"\"Build document vectors and compute norms for a chunk of documents.\"\"\"\n        doc_vectors = {}\n        doc_norms = {}\n\n        for doc_id in doc_ids:\n            tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n            doc_vector = {}\n            norm_squared = 0.0\n            for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n                norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n            doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n            doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n        return doc_vectors, doc_norms\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        vector_results = pool.map(_build_doc_vectors_chunk, doc_id_chunks)\n\n    # Import heapq here (each worker needs it)\n    from heapq import nlargest\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(\n            SearchResult(\n                doc_id=doc_id,\n                score=score,\n                title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n            )\n        )\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(queries, index, top_k=10, num_workers=None, documents=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (List of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary\n\n\ndef _merge_chunk_results(chunk_results):\n    \"\"\"\n    Merge results from all worker chunks.\n\n    Args:\n        chunk_results: List of (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary) tuples\n\n    Returns:\n        Merged (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    merged_doc_term_freqs = {}\n    merged_doc_terms = {}\n    merged_vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary in chunk_results:\n        merged_doc_term_freqs.update(doc_term_freqs)\n        merged_doc_terms.update(doc_terms)\n        merged_vocabulary.update(vocabulary)\n\n    return merged_doc_term_freqs, merged_doc_terms, merged_vocabulary\n\n\ndef _create_chunks(documents, chunk_size):\n    \"\"\"\n    Split documents into chunks.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of documents\n        chunk_size: Size of each chunk\n\n    Returns:\n        List of document chunks\n    \"\"\"\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(documents[i : i + chunk_size])\n    return chunks\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents, num_workers=None, chunk_size=500):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Steps:\n    1. Split documents into chunks\n    2. Process chunks in parallel (tokenize, compute TF)\n    3. Merge results from all chunks\n    4. Calculate document frequencies (sequential)\n    5. Compute IDF scores (sequential)\n    6. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores (parallel)\n    7. Compute document vector norms (parallel)\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Size of each chunk for processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # Determine number of workers\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Create chunks\n    chunks = _create_chunks(documents, chunk_size)\n\n    # Step 2: Process chunks in parallel (tokenize and compute TF)\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        chunk_results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks)\n\n    # Step 3: Merge results from all chunks\n    doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary = _merge_chunk_results(chunk_results)\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n    # Step 4: Calculate document frequencies (sequential - fast operation)\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 5: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast operation)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 6: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores (parallel)\n    # Prepare data for parallel processing\n    vocab_chunks = _create_chunks(list(index.vocabulary), chunk_size=1000)\n\n    def _build_inverted_index_chunk(terms):\n        \"\"\"Build inverted index for a subset of terms.\"\"\"\n        partial_index = {}\n        for term in terms:\n            posting_list = []\n            for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n                if term in tf_dict:\n                    tf = tf_dict[term]\n                    tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                    posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n            # Sort by score descending\n            posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n            partial_index[term] = posting_list\n        return partial_index\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        partial_indexes = pool.map(_build_inverted_index_chunk, vocab_chunks)\n\n    # Merge inverted indexes\n    for partial_index in partial_indexes:\n        index.inverted_index.update(partial_index)\n\n    # Step 7: Build document vectors and compute norms (parallel)\n    doc_id_chunks = _create_chunks(list(doc_term_freqs.keys()), chunk_size=1000)\n\n    def _build_doc_vectors_chunk(doc_ids):\n        \"\"\"Build document vectors and compute norms for a chunk of documents.\"\"\"\n        doc_vectors = {}\n        doc_norms = {}\n\n        for doc_id in doc_ids:\n            tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n            doc_vector = {}\n            norm_squared = 0.0\n            for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n                norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n            doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n            doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n        return doc_vectors, doc_norms\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        vector_results = pool.map(_build_doc_vectors_chunk, doc_id_chunks)\n\n    # Merge document vectors and norms\n    for doc_vectors, doc_norms in vector_results:\n        index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors)\n        index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(args):\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to search for a single query.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, index, top_k, documents)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, index, top_k, documents = args\n\n    # Import heapq here (each worker needs it)\n    from heapq import nlargest\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(\n            SearchResult(\n                doc_id=doc_id,\n                score=score,\n                title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n            )\n        )\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(queries, index, top_k=10, num_workers=None, documents=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (List of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # Determine number of workers\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    # Prepare arguments for each query\n    args_list = [(query, index, top_k, documents) for query in queries]\n\n    # Process queries in parallel\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_search_single_query, args_list)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point (for testing)\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    import argparse\n\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus, load_corpus\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-docs\",\n        type=int,\n        default=5000,\n        help=\"Number of documents to generate if no corpus provided\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-queries\", type=int, default=100, help=\"Number of queries for batch search\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results to return\")\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-workers\",\n        type=int,\n        default=None,\n        help=\"Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--chunk-size\", type=int, default=500, help=\"Chunk size for parallel processing\"\n    )\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Determine workers\n    num_workers = args.num_workers if args.num_workers else cpu_count()\n    print(f\"Using {num_workers} worker processes\")\n\n    # Build index in parallel\n    print(f\"\\nBuilding TF-IDF index in parallel (chunk_size={args.chunk_size})...\")\n    result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n        documents, num_workers=num_workers, chunk_size=args.chunk_size\n    )\n\n    print(f\"\\nIndex built in {result.elapsed_time:.3f} seconds\")\n    print(f\"Vocabulary size: {result.vocabulary_size:,} terms\")\n    print(f\"Documents indexed: {result.num_documents}\")\n\n    # Split documents into chunks\n    2. Process chunks in parallel (tokenize, compute TF)\n    3. Merge results from all chunks\n    4. Calculate document frequencies (sequential)\n    5. Compute IDF scores (sequential)\n    6. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores (parallel)\n    7. Compute document vector norms (parallel)\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Size of each chunk for processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # Determine number of workers\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Create chunks\n    chunks = _create_chunks(documents, chunk_size)\n\n    # Step 2: Process chunks in parallel (tokenize and compute TF)\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        chunk_results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks)\n\n    # Step 3: Merge results from all chunks\n    doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary = _merge_chunk_results(chunk_results)\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n    # Step 4: Calculate document frequencies (sequential - fast operation)\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 5: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast operation)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 6: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores (parallel)\n    # Prepare data for parallel processing\n    vocab_chunks = _create_chunks(list(index.vocabulary), chunk_size=1000)\n    inverted_index_args = [(chunk, doc_term_freqs, index.idf) for chunk in vocab_chunks]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        partial_indexes = pool.map(_build_inverted_index_chunk, inverted_index_args)\n\n    # Merge inverted indexes\n    for partial_index in partial_indexes:\n        index.inverted_index.update(partial_index)\n\n    # Step 7: Build document vectors and compute norms (parallel)\n    doc_id_chunks = _create_chunks(list(doc_term_freqs.keys()), chunk_size=1000)\n    doc_vector_args = [(chunk, doc_term_freqs, index.idf) for chunk in doc_id_chunks]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        vector_results = pool.map(_build_doc_vectors_chunk, doc_vector_args)\n\n    # Merge document vectors and norms\n    for doc_vectors, doc_norms in vector_results:\n        index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors)\n        index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - documents.\n\n    Args:\n        chunk_data: Tuple of (documents_chunk,)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    documents = chunk_data\n\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary\n\n\ndef _merge_chunk_results(chunk_results):\n    \"\"\"\n    Merge results from all worker chunks.\n\n    Args:\n        chunk_results: List of (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary) Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Steps:\n    1. Split documents into chunks\n    2. Process chunks in parallel (tokenize, compute TF)\n    3. Merge results from all chunks\n    4. Calculate document frequencies (sequential)\n    5. Compute IDF scores (sequential)\n    6. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores (parallel)\n    7. Compute document vector norms (parallel)\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Size of each chunk for processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # Determine number of workers\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Create chunks\n    chunks = _create_chunks(documents, chunk_size)\n\n    # Step 2: Process chunks in parallel (tokenize and compute TF)\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        chunk_results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks)\n\n    # Step 3: Merge results from all chunks\n    doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary = _merge_chunk_results(chunk_results)\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n    # Step 5: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast operation)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 6: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores (parallel)\n    # Prepare data for parallel processing\n    vocab_chunks = _create_chunks(list(index.vocabulary), chunk_size=1000)\n\n    def _build_inverted_index_chunk(terms):\n        \"\"\"Build inverted index for a subset of terms.\"\"\"\n        partial_index = {}\n        for term in terms:\n            posting_list = []\n            for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n                if term in tf_dict:\n                    tf = tf_dict[term]\n                    tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                    posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n            # Sort by score descending\n            posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n            partial_index[term] = posting_list\n        return partial_index\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        partial_indexes = pool.map(_build_inverted_index_chunk, vocab_chunks)\n\n    # Merge inverted indexes\n    for partial_index in partial_indexes:\n        index.inverted_index.update(partial_index)\n\n    # Step 7: Build document vectors and compute norms (parallel)\n    doc_id_chunks = _create_chunks(list(doc_term_freqs.keys()), chunk_size=1000)\n\n    def _build_doc_vectors_chunk(doc_ids):\n        \"\"\"Build document vectors and compute norms for a chunk of documents.\"\"\"\n        doc_vectors = {}\n        doc_norms = {}\n\n        for doc_id in doc_ids:\n            tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n            doc_vector = {}\n            norm_squared = 0.0\n            for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n                norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n            doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n            doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n        return doc_vectors, doc_norms\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        vector_results = pool.map(_build_doc_vectors_chunk, doc_id_chunks)\n\n    # Import heapq here (each worker needs it)\n    from heapq import nlargest\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(\n            SearchResult(\n                doc_id=doc_id,\n                score=score,\n                title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n            )\n        )\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(queries, index, top_k=10, num_workers=None, documents=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (List of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch similarity search using parallel query processing\n\nUses the multiprocessing Pool for CPU-bound parallel execution.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom multiprocessing import Pool, cpu_count\n\n# Import from sequential implementation\nfrom sequential import (\n    STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    Document,\n    IndexingResult,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk(chunk_data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to process a chunk of documents.\n\n    Args:\n        chunk_data: Tuple of (documents_chunk,)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    documents = chunk_data\n\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary\n\n\ndef _merge_chunk_results(chunk_results):\n    \"\"\"\n    Merge results from all worker chunks.\n\n    Args:\n        chunk_results: List of (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary) tuples\n\n    Returns:\n        Merged (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    merged_doc_term_freqs = {}\n    merged_doc_terms = {}\n    merged_vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary in chunk_results:\n        merged_doc_term_freqs.update(doc_term_freqs)\n        merged_doc_terms.update(doc_terms)\n        merged_vocabulary.update(vocabulary)\n\n    return merged_doc_term_freqs, merged_doc_terms, merged_vocabulary\n\n\ndef _create_chunks(documents, chunk_size):\n    \"\"\"\n    Split documents into chunks.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of documents\n        chunk_size: Size of each chunk\n\n    Returns:\n        List of document chunks\n    \"\"\"\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(documents[i : i + chunk_size])\n    return chunks\n\n\ndef _build_inverted_index_chunk(args):\n    \"\"\"Build inverted index for a subset of terms.\"\"\"\n    terms, doc_term_freqs, idf = args\n    partial_index = {}\n    for term in terms:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        # Sort by score descending\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        partial_index[term] = posting_list\n    return partial_index\n\n\ndef _build_doc_vectors_chunk(args):\n    \"\"\"Build document vectors and compute norms for a chunk of documents.\"\"\"\n    doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf = args\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n        tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return doc_vectors, doc_norms\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents, num_workers=None, chunk_size=500):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Steps:\n    1. Split documents into chunks\n    2. Process chunks in parallel (tokenize, compute TF)\n    3. Merge results from all chunks\n    4. Calculate document frequencies (sequential)\n    5. Compute IDF scores (sequential)\n    6. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores (parallel)\n    7. Compute document vector norms (parallel)\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Size of each chunk for processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # Determine number of workers\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Create chunks\n    chunks = _create_chunks(documents, chunk_size)\n\n    # Step 2: Process chunks in parallel (tokenize and compute TF)\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        chunk_results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks)\n\n    # Step 3: Merge results from all chunks\n    doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary = _merge_chunk_results(chunk_results)\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n    # Step 4: Calculate document frequencies (sequential - fast operation)\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 5: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast operation)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 6: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores (parallel)\n    # Prepare data for parallel processing\n    vocab_chunks = _create_chunks(list(index.vocabulary), chunk_size=1000)\n    inverted_index_args = [(chunk, doc_term_freqs, index.idf) for chunk in vocab_chunks]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        partial_indexes = pool.map(_build_inverted_index_chunk, inverted_index_args)\n\n    # Merge inverted indexes\n    for partial_index in partial_indexes:\n        index.inverted_index.update(partial_index)\n\n    # Step 7: Build document vectors and compute norms (parallel)\n    doc_id_chunks = _create_chunks(list(doc_term_freqs.keys()), chunk_size=1000)\n    doc_vector_args = [(chunk, doc_term_freqs, index.idf) for chunk in doc_id_chunks]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        vector_results = pool.map(_build_doc_vectors_chunk, doc_vector_args)\n\n    # Merge document vectors and norms\n    for doc_vectors, doc_norms in vector_results:\n        index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors)\n        index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(args):\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to search for a single query.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, index, top_k, documents)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, index, top_k, documents = args\n\n    # Import heapq here (each worker needs it)\n    from heapq import nlargest\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(\n            SearchResult(\n                doc_id=doc_id,\n                score=score,\n                title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n            )\n        )\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(queries, index, top_k=10, num_workers=None, documents=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (List of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # Determine number of workers\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    # Prepare arguments for each query\n    args_list = [(query, index, top_k, documents) for query in queries]\n\n    # TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch similarity search using parallel query processing\n\nUses the multiprocessing Pool for CPU-bound parallel execution.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom multiprocessing import Pool, cpu_count\n\n# Import from sequential implementation\nfrom sequential import (\n    STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    Document,\n    IndexingResult,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk(chunk_data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to process a chunk of documents.\n\n    Args:\n        chunk_data: Tuple of (documents_chunk,)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    documents = chunk_data\n\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary\n\n\ndef _merge_chunk_results(chunk_results):\n    \"\"\"\n    Merge results from all worker chunks.\n\n    Args:\n        chunk_results: List of (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary) tuples\n\n    Returns:\n        Merged (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    merged_doc_term_freqs = {}\n    merged_doc_terms = {}\n    merged_vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary in chunk_results:\n        merged_doc_term_freqs.update(doc_term_freqs)\n        merged_doc_terms.update(doc_terms)\n        merged_vocabulary.update(vocabulary)\n\n    return merged_doc_term_freqs, merged_doc_terms, merged_vocabulary\n\n\ndef _create_chunks(documents, chunk_size):\n    \"\"\"\n    Split documents into chunks.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of documents\n        chunk_size: Size of each chunk\n\n    Returns:\n        List of document chunks\n    \"\"\"\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(documents[i : i + chunk_size])\n    return chunks\n\n\ndef _compute_tfidf_for_docs(args):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute TF-IDF vectors and norms for a chunk of documents.\n    This combines inverted index building and doc vector computation.\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_chunk_data, idf = args\n\n    # doc_chunk_data is list of (doc_id, tf_dict) tuples\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n    inverted_index_contributions = defaultdict(list)\n\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_chunk_data:\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n            # Contribute to inverted index\n            inverted_index_contributions[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return doc_vectors, doc_norms, inverted_index_contributions\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents, num_workers=None, chunk_size=500):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Steps:\n    1. Split documents into chunks\n    2. Process chunks in parallel (tokenize, compute TF)\n    3. Merge results from all chunks\n    4. Calculate document frequencies (sequential)\n    5. Compute IDF scores (sequential)\n    6. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores (parallel)\n    7. Compute document vector norms (parallel)\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Size of each chunk for processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # Determine number of workers\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Create chunks\n    chunks = _create_chunks(documents, chunk_size)\n\n    # Step 2: Process chunks in parallel (tokenize and compute TF)\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        chunk_results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks)\n\n    # Step 3: Merge results from all chunks\n    doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary = _merge_chunk_results(chunk_results)\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n    # Step 4: Calculate document frequencies (sequential - fast operation)\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 5: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast operation)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 6: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores (parallel)\n    # Prepare data for parallel processing\n    vocab_chunks = _create_chunks(list(index.vocabulary), chunk_size=1000)\n    inverted_index_args = [(chunk, doc_term_freqs, index.idf) for chunk in vocab_chunks]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        partial_indexes = pool.map(_build_inverted_index_chunk, inverted_index_args)\n\n    # Merge inverted indexes\n    for partial_index in partial_indexes:\n        index.inverted_index.update(partial_index)\n\n    # Step 7: Build document vectors and compute norms (parallel)\n    doc_id_chunks = _create_chunks(list(doc_term_freqs.keys()), chunk_size=1000)\n    doc_vector_args = [(chunk, doc_term_freqs, index.idf) for chunk in doc_id_chunks]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        vector_results = pool.map(_build_doc_vectors_chunk, doc_vector_args)\n\n    # Merge document vectors and norms\n    for doc_vectors, doc_norms in vector_results:\n        index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors)\n        index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(args):\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to search for a single query.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, index, top_k, documents)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, index, top_k, documents = args\n\n    # Import heapq here (each worker needs it)\n    from heapq import nlargest\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(\n            SearchResult(\n                doc_id=doc_id,\n                score=score,\n                title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n            )\n        )\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(queries, index, top_k=10, num_workers=None, documents=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (List of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch similarity search using parallel query processing\n\nUses the multiprocessing Pool for CPU-bound parallel execution.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom multiprocessing import Pool, cpu_count\n\n# Import from sequential implementation\nfrom sequential import (\n    STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    Document,\n    IndexingResult,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk(chunk_data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to process a chunk of documents.\n\n    Args:\n        chunk_data: Tuple of (documents_chunk,)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    documents = chunk_data\n\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary\n\n\ndef _merge_chunk_results(chunk_results):\n    \"\"\"\n    Merge results from all worker chunks.\n\n    Args:\n        chunk_results: List of (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary) tuples\n\n    Returns:\n        Merged (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    merged_doc_term_freqs = {}\n    merged_doc_terms = {}\n    merged_vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary in chunk_results:\n        merged_doc_term_freqs.update(doc_term_freqs)\n        merged_doc_terms.update(doc_terms)\n        merged_vocabulary.update(vocabulary)\n\n    return merged_doc_term_freqs, merged_doc_terms, merged_vocabulary\n\n\ndef _create_chunks(documents, chunk_size):\n    \"\"\"\n    Split documents into chunks.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of documents\n        chunk_size: Size of each chunk\n\n    Returns:\n        List of document chunks\n    \"\"\"\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(documents[i : i + chunk_size])\n    return chunks\n\n\ndef _compute_tfidf_for_docs(args):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute TF-IDF vectors and norms for a chunk of documents.\n    This combines inverted index building and doc vector computation.\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_chunk_data, idf = args\n\n    # doc_chunk_data is list of (doc_id, tf_dict) tuples\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n    inverted_index_contributions = defaultdict(list)\n\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_chunk_data:\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n            # Contribute to inverted index\n            inverted_index_contributions[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return doc_vectors, doc_norms, inverted_index_contributions\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents, num_workers=None, chunk_size=500):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Steps:\n    1. Split documents into chunks\n    2. Process chunks in parallel (tokenize, compute TF)\n    3. Merge results from all chunks\n    4. Calculate document frequencies (sequential)\n    5. Compute IDF scores (sequential)\n    6. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores (parallel)\n    7. Compute document vector norms (parallel)\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Size of each chunk for processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # Determine number of workers\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Create chunks\n    chunks = _create_chunks(documents, chunk_size)\n\n    # Step 2: Process chunks in parallel (tokenize and compute TF)\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        chunk_results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks)\n\n    # Step 3: Merge results from all chunks\n    doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary = _merge_chunk_results(chunk_results)\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n    # Step 4: Calculate document frequencies (sequential - fast operation)\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 5: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast operation)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 6 & 7: Build TF-IDF vectors, norms, and inverted index (parallel)\n    # Prepare document chunks with their TF data\n    doc_items = list(doc_term_freqs.items())\n    doc_chunks = _create_chunks(doc_items, chunk_size)\n    tfidf_args = [(chunk, index.idf) for chunk in doc_chunks]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        tfidf_results = pool.map(_compute_tfidf_for_docs, tfidf_args)\n\n    # Merge results\n    for doc_vectors, doc_norms, inverted_contributions in tfidf_results:\n        index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors)\n        index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms)\n\n        # Merge inverted index contributions\n        for term, postings in inverted_contributions.items():\n            if term not in index.inverted_index:\n                index.inverted_index[term] = []\n            index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n\n    # Sort inverted index posting lists by score\n    for term in index.inverted_index:\n        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(args):\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to search for a single query.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, index, top_k, documents)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, index, top_k, documents = args\n\n    # Import heapq here (each worker needs it)\n    from heapq import nlargest\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(\n            SearchResult(\n                doc_id=doc_id,\n                score=score,\n                title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n            )\n        )\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(queries, index, top_k=10, num_workers=None, documents=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (List of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # Determine number of workers\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    # Prepare arguments for each query\n    args_list = [(query, index, top_k, documents) for query in queries]\n\n    #
