TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch similarity search using parallel query processing\n\nUses the multiprocessing Pool for CPU-bound parallel execution.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom multiprocessing import Pool, cpu_count\n\n# Import from sequential implementation\nfrom sequential import (\n    STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    Document,\n    IndexingResult,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk(chunk_data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to process a chunk of documents.\n\n    Args:\n        chunk_data: Tuple of (documents_chunk,)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    documents = chunk_data\n\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary\n\n\ndef _merge_chunk_results(chunk_results):\n    \"\"\"\n    Merge results from all worker chunks.\n\n    Args:\n        chunk_results: List of (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary) tuples\n\n    Returns:\n        Merged (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    merged_doc_term_freqs = {}\n    merged_doc_terms = {}\n    merged_vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary in chunk_results:\n        merged_doc_term_freqs.update(doc_term_freqs)\n        merged_doc_terms.update(doc_terms)\n        merged_vocabulary.update(vocabulary)\n\n    return merged_doc_term_freqs, merged_doc_terms, merged_vocabulary\n\n\ndef _create_chunks(documents, chunk_size):\n    \"\"\"\n    Split documents into chunks.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of documents\n        chunk_size: Size of each chunk\n\n    Returns:\n        List of document chunks\n    \"\"\"\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(documents[i : i + chunk_size])\n    return chunks\n\n\ndef _compute_tfidf_for_docs(args):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute TF-IDF vectors and norms for a chunk of documents.\n    This combines inverted index building and doc vector computation.\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_chunk_data, idf = args\n\n    # doc_chunk_data is list of (doc_id, tf_dict) tuples\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n    inverted_index_contributions = defaultdict(list)\n\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_chunk_data:\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n            # Contribute to inverted index\n            inverted_index_contributions[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return doc_vectors, doc_norms, inverted_index_contributions\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents, num_workers=None, chunk_size=500):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Steps:\n    1. Split documents into chunks\n    2. Process chunks in parallel (tokenize, compute TF)\n    3. Merge results from all chunks\n    4. Calculate document frequencies (sequential)\n    5. Compute IDF scores (sequential)\n    6. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores (parallel)\n    7. Compute document vector norms (parallel)\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Size of each chunk for processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # Determine number of workers\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Create chunks\n    chunks = _create_chunks(documents, chunk_size)\n\n    # Step 2: Process chunks in parallel (tokenize and compute TF)\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        chunk_results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks)\n\n    # Step 3: Merge results from all chunks\n    doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary = _merge_chunk_results(chunk_results)\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n    # Step 4: Calculate document frequencies (sequential - fast operation)\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 5: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast operation)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 6 & 7: Build TF-IDF vectors, norms, and inverted index (parallel)\n    # Prepare document chunks with their TF data\n    doc_items = list(doc_term_freqs.items())\n    doc_chunks = _create_chunks(doc_items, chunk_size)\n    tfidf_args = [(chunk, index.idf) for chunk in doc_chunks]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        tfidf_results = pool.map(_compute_tfidf_for_docs, tfidf_args)\n\n    # Merge results\n    for doc_vectors, doc_norms, inverted_contributions in tfidf_results:\n        index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors)\n        index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms)\n\n        # Merge inverted index contributions\n        for term, postings in inverted_contributions.items():\n            if term not in index.inverted_index:\n                index.inverted_index[term] = []\n            index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n\n    # Sort inverted index posting lists by score\n    for term in index.inverted_index:\n        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n# Global variables for worker processes (set via initializer)\n_worker_index = None\n_worker_doc_titles = None\n\n\ndef _init_search_worker(index, doc_titles):\n    \"\"\"Initialize worker process with shared index and document titles.\"\"\"\n    global _worker_index, _worker_doc_titles\n    _worker_index = index\n    _worker_doc_titles = doc_titles\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(args):\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to search for a single query using pre-loaded index.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, top_k)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, top_k = args\n\n    # Use global index (set via initializer)\n    index = _worker_index\n    doc_titles = _worker_doc_titles\n\n    # Import heapq here (each worker needs it)\n    from heapq import nlargest\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(\n            SearchResult(\n                doc_id=doc_id,\n                score=score,\n                title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n            )\n        )\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(queries, index, top_k=10, num_workers=None, documents=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (List of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # Determine number of workers\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    # Prepare arguments for each query\n    args_list = [(query, index, top_k, documents) for query in queries]\n\n    # Process queries in parallel\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_search_single_query, args_list)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point (for testing)\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    import argparse\n\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus, load_corpus\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-docs\",\n        type=int,\n        default=5000,\n        help=\"Number of documents to generate if no corpus provided\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-queries\", type=int, default=100, help=\"Number of queries for batch search\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results to return\")\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-workers\",\n        type=int,\n        default=None,\n        help=\"Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--chunk-size\", type=int, default=500, help=\"Chunk size for parallel processing\"\n    )\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch similarity search using parallel query processing\n\nUses the multiprocessing Pool for CPU-bound parallel execution.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom multiprocessing import Pool, cpu_count\n\n# Import from sequential implementation\nfrom sequential import (\n    STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    Document,\n    IndexingResult,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk(chunk_data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to process a chunk of documents.\n\n    Args:\n        chunk_data: Tuple of (documents_chunk,)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    documents = chunk_data\n\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary\n\n\ndef _merge_chunk_results(chunk_results):\n    \"\"\"\n    Merge results from all worker chunks.\n\n    Args:\n        chunk_results: List of (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary) tuples\n\n    Returns:\n        Merged (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    merged_doc_term_freqs = {}\n    merged_doc_terms = {}\n    merged_vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary in chunk_results:\n        merged_doc_term_freqs.update(doc_term_freqs)\n        merged_doc_terms.update(doc_terms)\n        merged_vocabulary.update(vocabulary)\n\n    return merged_doc_term_freqs, merged_doc_terms, merged_vocabulary\n\n\ndef _create_chunks(documents, chunk_size):\n    \"\"\"\n    Split documents into chunks.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of documents\n        chunk_size: Size of each chunk\n\n    Returns:\n        List of document chunks\n    \"\"\"\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(documents[i : i + chunk_size])\n    return chunks\n\n\ndef _compute_tfidf_for_docs(args):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute TF-IDF vectors and norms for a chunk of documents.\n    This combines inverted index building and doc vector computation.\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_chunk_data, idf = args\n\n    # doc_chunk_data is list of (doc_id, tf_dict) tuples\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n    inverted_index_contributions = defaultdict(list)\n\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_chunk_data:\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n            # Contribute to inverted index\n            inverted_index_contributions[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return doc_vectors, doc_norms, inverted_index_contributions\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents, num_workers=None, chunk_size=500):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Steps:\n    1. Split documents into chunks\n    2. Process chunks in parallel (tokenize, compute TF)\n    3. Merge results from all chunks\n    4. Calculate document frequencies (sequential)\n    5. Compute IDF scores (sequential)\n    6. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores (parallel)\n    7. Compute document vector norms (parallel)\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Size of each chunk for processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # Determine number of workers\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Create chunks\n    chunks = _create_chunks(documents, chunk_size)\n\n    # Step 2: Process chunks in parallel (tokenize and compute TF)\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        chunk_results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks)\n\n    # Step 3: Merge results from all chunks\n    doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary = _merge_chunk_results(chunk_results)\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n    # Step 4: Calculate document frequencies (sequential - fast operation)\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 5: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast operation)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 6 & 7: Build TF-IDF vectors, norms, and inverted index (parallel)\n    # Prepare document chunks with their TF data\n    doc_items = list(doc_term_freqs.items())\n    doc_chunks = _create_chunks(doc_items, chunk_size)\n    tfidf_args = [(chunk, index.idf) for chunk in doc_chunks]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        tfidf_results = pool.map(_compute_tfidf_for_docs, tfidf_args)\n\n    # Merge results\n    for doc_vectors, doc_norms, inverted_contributions in tfidf_results:\n        index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors)\n        index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms)\n\n        # Merge inverted index contributions\n        for term, postings in inverted_contributions.items():\n            if term not in index.inverted_index:\n                index.inverted_index[term] = []\n            index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n\n    # Sort inverted index posting lists by score\n    for term in index.inverted_index:\n        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n# Global variables for worker processes (set via initializer)\n_worker_index = None\n_worker_doc_titles = None\n\n\ndef _init_search_worker(index, doc_titles):\n    \"\"\"Initialize worker process with shared index and document titles.\"\"\"\n    global _worker_index, _worker_doc_titles\n    _worker_index = index\n    _worker_doc_titles = doc_titles\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(args):\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to search for a single query using pre-loaded index.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, top_k)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, top_k = args\n\n    # Use global index (set via initializer)\n    index = _worker_index\n    doc_titles = _worker_doc_titles\n\n    # Import heapq here (each worker needs it)\n    from heapq import nlargest\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # len(documents), chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(documents[i : i + chunk_size])\n    return chunks\n\n\ndef _compute_tfidf_for_docs(args):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute TF-IDF vectors and norms for a chunk of documents.\n    This combines inverted index building and doc vector computation.\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_chunk_data, idf = args\n\n    # doc_chunk_data is list of (doc_id, tf_dict) tuples\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n    inverted_index_contributions = defaultdict(list)\n\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_chunk_data:\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n            # Contribute to inverted index\n            inverted_index_contributions[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return doc_vectors, doc_norms, inverted_index_contributions\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents, num_workers=None, chunk_size=500):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Steps:\n    1. Split documents into chunks\n    2. Process chunks in parallel (tokenize, compute TF)\n    3. Merge results from all chunks\n    4. Calculate document frequencies (sequential)\n    5. Compute IDF scores (sequential)\n    6. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores (parallel)\n    7. Compute document vector norms (parallel)\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Size of each chunk for processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # Determine number of workers\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Create larger chunks to reduce overhead\n    # Use fewer chunks (num_workers * 2) for better performance\n    optimal_chunk_size = max(chunk_size, len(documents) // (num_workers * 2))\n    chunks = _create_chunks(documents, optimal_chunk_size)\n\n    # Step 2: Process chunks in parallel (tokenize and compute TF)\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        chunk_results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks)\n\n    # Step 3: Merge results from all chunks\n    doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary = _merge_chunk_results(chunk_results)\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n    # Step 4: Calculate document frequencies (sequential - fast operation)\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 5: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast operation)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 6 & 7: Build TF-IDF vectors, norms, and inverted index (parallel)\n    # Prepare document chunks with their TF data\n    doc_items = list(doc_term_freqs.items())\n    doc_chunks = _create_chunks(doc_items, optimal_chunk_size)\n    tfidf_args = [(chunk, index.idf) for chunk in doc_chunks]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        tfidf_results = pool.map(_compute_tfidf_for_docs, tfidf_args)\n\n    # Merge results\n    for doc_vectors, doc_norms, inverted_contributions in tfidf_results:\n        index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors)\n        index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms)\n\n        # Merge inverted index contributions\n        for term, postings in inverted_contributions.items():\n            if term not in index.inverted_index:\n                index.inverted_index[term] = []\n            index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n\n    # Sort inverted index posting lists by score\n    for term in index.inverted_index:\n        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n# Global variables for worker processes (set via initializer)\n_worker_index = None\n_worker_doc_titles = None\n\n\ndef _init_search_worker(index, doc_titles):\n    \"\"\"Initialize worker process with shared index and document titles.\"\"\"\n    global _worker_index, _worker_doc_titles\n    _worker_index = index\n    _worker_doc_titles = doc_titles\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(args):\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to search for a single query using pre-loaded index.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, top_k)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, top_k = args\n\n    # Use global index (set via initializer)\n    index = _worker_index\n    doc_titles = _worker_doc_titles\n\n    # Import heapq here (each worker needs it)\n    from heapq import nlargest\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(\n            SearchResult(\n                doc_id=doc_id,\n                score=score,\n                title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n            )\n        )\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(queries, index, top_k=10, num_workers=None, documents=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (List of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    #   Document,\n    IndexingResult,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n\n# chunk of documents.\n    This combines inverted index building and doc vector computation.\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_chunk_data, idf = args\n\n    # doc_chunk_data is list of (doc_id, tf_dict) tuples\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n    inverted_index_contributions = defaultdict(list)\n\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_chunk_data:\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n            # Contribute to inverted index\n            inverted_index_contributions[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return doc_vectors, 1. Split documents into chunks\n    2. Process chunks in parallel (tokenize, compute TF)\n    3. Merge results from all chunks\n    4. Calculate document frequencies (sequential)\n    5. Compute IDF scores (sequential)\n    6. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores (parallel)\n    7. Compute document vector norms (parallel)\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Size of each chunk for processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # Determine number of workers\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Create larger chunks to reduce overhead\n    # Use fewer chunks (num_workers * 2) for better performance\n    optimal_chunk_size = max(chunk_size, len(documents) // (num_workers * 2))\n    chunks = _create_chunks(documents, optimal_chunk_size)\n\n    # Step 2: Process chunks in parallel (tokenize and compute TF)\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        chunk_results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks, chunksize=1)\n\n    # Step 3: Merge results from all chunks\n    doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary = _merge_chunk_results(chunk_results)\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n    # Step 4: Calculate document frequencies (sequential - fast operation)\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 5: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast operation)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 6 & 7: Build TF-IDF vectors, norms, and inverted index (parallel)\n    # Prepare document chunks with their TF data\n    doc_items = list(doc_term_freqs.items())\n    doc_chunks = _create_chunks(doc_items, optimal_chunk_size)\n    tfidf_args = [(chunk, index.idf) for chunk in doc_chunks]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        tfidf_results = pool.map(_compute_tfidf_for_docs, tfidf_args)\n\n    # Merge results\n    for doc_vectors, doc_norms, inverted_contributions in tfidf_results:\n        index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors)\n        index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms)\n\n        # Merge inverted index contributions\n        for term, postings in inverted_contributions.items():\n            if term not in index.inverted_index:\n                index.inverted_index[term] = []\n            index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n\n    # Sort inverted index posting lists by score\n    for term in index.inverted_index:\n        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n# Global variables for worker processes (set via initializer)\n_worker_index = None\n_worker_doc_titles = None\n\n\ndef _init_search_worker(index, doc_titles):\n    \"\"\"Initialize worker process with shared index and document titles.\"\"\"\n    global _worker_index, _worker_doc_titles\n    _worker_index = index\n    _worker_doc_titles = doc_titles\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(args):\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to search for a single query using pre-loaded index.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, top_k)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, top_k = args\n\n    # Use global index (set via initializer)\n    index = _worker_index\n    doc_titles = _worker_doc_titles\n\n    # Import heapq here (each worker needs it)\n    from heapq import nlargest\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(\n            SearchResult(\n                doc_id=doc_id,\n                score=score,\n                title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n            )\n        )\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(queries, index, top_k=10, num_workers=None, documents=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (List of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch similarity search using parallel query processing\n\nUses the multiprocessing Pool for CPU-bound parallel execution.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom multiprocessing import Pool, cpu_count\n\n# Import from sequential implementation\nfrom sequential import (\n    STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    Document,\n    IndexingResult,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk(chunk_data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to process a chunk of documents.\n\n    Args:\n        chunk_data: Tuple of (documents_chunk,)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    documents = chunk_data\n\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary\n\n\ndef _merge_chunk_results(chunk_results):\n    \"\"\"\n    Merge results from all worker chunks.\n\n    Args:\n        chunk_results: List of (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary) tuples\n\n    Returns:\n        Merged (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    merged_doc_term_freqs = {}\n    merged_doc_terms = {}\n    merged_vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary in chunk_results:\n        merged_doc_term_freqs.update(doc_term_freqs)\n        merged_doc_terms.update(doc_terms)\n        merged_vocabulary.update(vocabulary)\n\n    return merged_doc_term_freqs, merged_doc_terms, merged_vocabulary\n\n\ndef _create_chunks(documents, chunk_size):\n    \"\"\"\n    Split documents into chunks.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of documents\n        chunk_size: Size of each chunk\n\n    Returns:\n        List of document chunks\n    \"\"\"\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(documents[i : i + chunk_size])\n    return chunks\n\n\ndef _compute_tfidf_for_docs(args):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute TF-IDF vectors and norms for a chunk of documents.\n    This combines inverted index building and doc vector computation.\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_chunk_data, idf = args\n\n    # doc_chunk_data is list of (doc_id, tf_dict) tuples\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n    inverted_index_contributions = defaultdict(list)\n\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_chunk_data:\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n            # Contribute to inverted index\n            inverted_index_contributions[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return doc_vectors, doc_norms, inverted_index_contributions\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents, num_workers=None, chunk_size=500):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Steps:\n    1. Split documents into chunks\n    2. Process chunks in parallel (tokenize, compute TF)\n    3. Merge results from all chunks\n    4. Calculate document frequencies (sequential)\n    5. Compute IDF scores (sequential)\n    6. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores (parallel)\n    7. Compute document vector norms (parallel)\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Size of each chunk for processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # Determine number of workers\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Create larger chunks to reduce overhead\n    # Use fewer chunks (num_workers * 2) for better performance\n    optimal_chunk_size = max(chunk_size, len(documents) // (num_workers * 2))\n    chunks = _create_chunks(documents, optimal_chunk_size)\n\n    # Step 2: Process chunks in parallel (tokenize and compute TF)\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        chunk_results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks, chunksize=1)\n\n    # Step 3: Merge results from all chunks\n    doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary = _merge_chunk_results(chunk_results)\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n    # Step 4: Calculate document frequencies (sequential - fast operation)\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 5: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast operation)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 6 & 7: Build TF-IDF vectors, norms, and inverted index (parallel)\n    # Prepare document chunks with their TF data\n    doc_items = list(doc_term_freqs.items())\n    doc_chunks = _create_chunks(doc_items, optimal_chunk_size)\n    tfidf_args = [(chunk, index.idf) for chunk in doc_chunks]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        tfidf_results = pool.map(_compute_tfidf_for_docs, tfidf_args, chunksize=1)\n\n    # Merge results\n    for doc_vectors, doc_norms, inverted_contributions in tfidf_results:\n        index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors)\n        index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms)\n\n        # Merge inverted index contributions\n        for term, postings in inverted_contributions.items():\n            if term not in index.inverted_index:\n                index.inverted_index[term] = []\n            index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n\n    # Sort inverted index posting lists by score\n    for term in index.inverted_index:\n        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n# Global variables for worker processes (set via initializer)\n_worker_index = None\n_worker_doc_titles = None\n\n\ndef _init_search_worker(index, doc_titles):\n    \"\"\"Initialize worker process with shared index and document titles.\"\"\"\n    global _worker_index, _worker_doc_titles\n    _worker_index = index\n    _worker_doc_titles = doc_titles\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(args):\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to search for a single query using pre-loaded index.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, top_k)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, top_k = args\n\n    # Use global index (set via initializer)\n    index = _worker_index\n    doc_titles = _worker_doc_titles\n\n    # Import heapq here (each worker needs it)\n    from heapq import nlargest\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(\n            SearchResult(\n                doc_id=doc_id,\n                score=score,\n                title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n            )\n        )\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(queries, index, top_k=10, num_workers=None, documents=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (List of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # Determine number of workers\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    # Prepare document titles dictionary\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Prepare arguments for each query (now just query and top_k)\n    args_list = [(query, top_k) for query in queries]\n\n    # Process queries in parallel with index pre-loaded in each worker\n    with Pool(\n        processes=num_workers,\n        initializer=_init_search_worker,\n        initargs=(index, doc_titles),\n    ) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_search_single_query, args_list)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point (for testing)\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    import argparse\n\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus, load_corpus\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-docs\",\n        type=int,\n        default=5000,\n        help=\"Number of documents to generate if no corpus provided\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-queries\", type=int, default=100, help=\"Number of queries for batch search\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results to return\")\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-workers\",\n        type=int,\n        default=None,\n        help=\"Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--chunk-size\", type=int, default=500, help=\"Chunk size for parallel processing\"\n    )\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load or generate corpus\n    if args.corpus:\n        print(f\"\\nLoading corpus from {args.corpus}...\")\n        documents = load_corpus(args.corpus)\n    else:\n        print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n        documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n\n    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n    total_words = sum(d.word_count for d in documents)\n    print(f\"Total words: {total_words:,}\")\n\n    # Determine workers\n    num_workers = args.num_workers if args.num_workers else cpu_count()\n    print(f\"Using {num_workers} worker processes\")\n\n    # Build index in parallel\n    print(f\"\\nBuilding TF-IDF index in parallel (chunk_size={args.chunk_size})...\")\n    result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n        documents, num_workers=num_workers, chunk_size=args.chunk_size\n    )\n\n    print(f\"\\nIndex built in {result.elapsed_time:.3f} seconds\")\n    print(f\"Vocabulary size: {result.vocabulary_size:,} terms\")\n    print(f\"Documents indexed: {result.num_documents}\")\n\n    # Generate test queries\n    print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_queries} test queries...\")\n    import random\n\n    random.seed(42)\n    query_templates = [\n        \"machine learning algorithm\",\n        \"data processing system\",\n        \"network security protocol\",\n        \"database optimization technique\",\n        \"software development framework\",\n        \"cloud computing infrastructure\",\n        \"artificial intelligence model\",\n        \"distributed system architecture\",\n        \"medical diagnosis treatment\",\n        \"business strategy analysis\",\n    ]\n    queries = [random.choice(query_templates) for _ in range(args.num_queries)]\n\n    # Perform batch search in parallel\n    print(f\"\\nPerforming batch search ({args.num_queries} queries in parallel)...\")\n    results, search_time = batch_search_parallel(\n        queries, result.index, args.top_k, num_workers, documents\n    )\n\n    print(f\"Batch search completed in {search_time:.3f} seconds\")\n    print(f\"Average time per query: {search_time / len(queries) * 1000:.2f} ms\")\n\n    # TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch similarity search using parallel query processing\n\nUses the multiprocessing Pool for CPU-bound parallel execution.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom multiprocessing import Pool, cpu_count\n\n# Import from sequential implementation\nfrom sequential import (\n    STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    Document,\n    IndexingResult,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk(chunk_data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to process a chunk of documents.\n\n    Args:\n        chunk_data: Tuple of (documents_chunk,)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    documents = chunk_data\n\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary\n\n\ndef _merge_chunk_results(chunk_results):\n    \"\"\"\n    Merge results from all worker chunks.\n\n    Args:\n        chunk_results: List of (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary) tuples\n\n    Returns:\n        Merged (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    merged_doc_term_freqs = {}\n    merged_doc_terms = {}\n    merged_vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary in chunk_results:\n        merged_doc_term_freqs.update(doc_term_freqs)\n        merged_doc_terms.update(doc_terms)\n        merged_vocabulary.update(vocabulary)\n\n    return merged_doc_term_freqs, merged_doc_terms, merged_vocabulary\n\n\ndef _create_chunks(documents, chunk_size):\n    \"\"\"\n    Split documents into chunks.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of documents\n        chunk_size: Size of each chunk\n\n    Returns:\n        List of document chunks\n    \"\"\"\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(documents[i : i + chunk_size])\n    return chunks\n\n\ndef _compute_tfidf_for_docs(args):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute TF-IDF vectors and norms for a chunk of documents.\n    This combines inverted index building and doc vector computation.\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_chunk_data, idf = args\n\n    # doc_chunk_data is list of (doc_id, tf_dict) tuples\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n    inverted_index_contributions = defaultdict(list)\n\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_chunk_data:\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n            # Contribute to inverted index\n            inverted_index_contributions[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return doc_vectors, doc_norms, inverted_index_contributions\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents, num_workers=None, chunk_size=500):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Steps:\n    1. Split documents into chunks\n    2. Process chunks in parallel (tokenize, compute TF)\n    3. Merge results from all chunks\n    4. Calculate document frequencies (sequential)\n    5. Compute IDF scores (sequential)\n    6. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores (parallel)\n    7. Compute document vector norms (parallel)\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Size of each chunk for processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # Determine number of workers\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Create larger chunks to reduce overhead\n    # Use fewer chunks (num_workers * 2) for better performance\n    optimal_chunk_size = max(chunk_size, len(documents) // (num_workers * 2))\n    chunks = _create_chunks(documents, optimal_chunk_size)\n\n    # Step 2: Process chunks in parallel (tokenize and compute TF)\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        chunk_results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks, chunksize=1)\n\n    # Step 3: Merge results from all chunks\n    doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary = _merge_chunk_results(chunk_results)\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n    # Step 4: Calculate document frequencies (sequential - fast operation)\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 5: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast operation)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 6 & 7: Build TF-IDF vectors, norms, and inverted index (parallel)\n    # Prepare document chunks with their TF data\n    doc_items = list(doc_term_freqs.items())\n    doc_chunks = _create_chunks(doc_items, optimal_chunk_size)\n    tfidf_args = [(chunk, index.idf) for chunk in doc_chunks]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        tfidf_results = pool.map(_compute_tfidf_for_docs, tfidf_args, chunksize=1)\n\n    # Merge results\n    for doc_vectors, doc_norms, inverted_contributions in tfidf_results:\n        index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors)\n        index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms)\n\n        # Merge inverted index contributions\n        for term, postings in inverted_contributions.items():\n            if term not in index.inverted_index:\n                index.inverted_index[term] = []\n            index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n\n    # Sort inverted index posting lists by score\n    for term in index.inverted_index:\n        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n# Global variables for worker processes (set via initializer)\n_worker_index = None\n_worker_doc_titles = None\n\n\ndef _init_search_worker(index, doc_titles):\n    \"\"\"Initialize worker process with shared index and document titles.\"\"\"\n    global _worker_index, _worker_doc_titles\n    _worker_index = index\n    _worker_doc_titles = doc_titles\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(args):\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to search for a single query using pre-loaded index.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, top_k)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, top_k = args\n\n    # Use global index (set via initializer)\n    index = _worker_index\n    doc_titles = _worker_doc_titles\n\n    # Import heapq here (each worker needs it)\n    from heapq import nlargest\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(\n            SearchResult(\n                doc_id=doc_id,\n                score=score,\n                title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n            )\n        )\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(queries, index, top_k=10, num_workers=None, documents=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (List of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # Determine number of workers\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    # Prepare document titles dictionary\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Prepare arguments for each query (now just query and top_k)\n    args_list = [(query, top_k) for query in queries]\n\n    # Process queries in parallel with index pre-loaded in each worker\n    # Use smaller chunksize for better load balancing across queries\n    optimal_chunksize = max(1, len(queries) // (num_workers * 4))\n    with Pool(\n        processes=num_workers,\n        initializer=_init_search_worker,\n        initargs=(index, doc_titles),\n    ) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_search_single_query, args_list, chunksize=optimal_chunksize)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point (for testing)\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    import argparse\n\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus, load_corpus\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-docs\",\n        type=int,\n        default=5000,\n        help=\"Number of documents to generate if no corpus provided\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-queries\", type=int, default=100, help=\"Number of queries for batch search\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results to return\")\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-workers\",\n        type=int,\n        default=None,\n        help=\"Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--chunk-size\", type=int, default=500, help=\"Chunk size for parallel processing\"\n    )\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # [{res.score:.4f}] {res.title}\")\n\n    return result\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":174,"oldLines":31,"newStart":174,"newLines":31,"lines":["     optimal_chunk_size = max(chunk_size, len(documents) // (num_workers * 2))","     chunks = _create_chunks(documents, optimal_chunk_size)"," ","-    # Step 2: Process chunks in parallel (tokenize and compute TF)","+    # Single Pool for all parallel operations to reduce overhead","     with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:","+        # Step 2: Process chunks in parallel (tokenize and compute TF)","         chunk_results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks, chunksize=1)"," ","-    # Step 3: Merge results from all chunks","-    doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary = _merge_chunk_results(chunk_results)","-    index.vocabulary = vocabulary","+        # Step 3: Merge results from all chunks","+        doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary = _merge_chunk_results(chunk_results)","+        index.vocabulary = vocabulary"," ","-    # Step 4: Calculate document frequencies (sequential - documents.\n\n    Args:\n        chunk_data: Tuple of (documents_chunk,)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    documents = chunk_data\n\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary\n\n\ndef _merge_chunk_results(chunk_results):\n    \"\"\"\n    Merge results from all worker chunks.\n\n    Args:\n        chunk_results: List of (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary) tuples\n\n    Returns:\n        Merged (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    merged_doc_term_freqs = {}\n    merged_doc_terms = {}\n    merged_vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary in chunk_results:\n        merged_doc_term_freqs.update(doc_term_freqs)\n        merged_doc_terms.update(doc_terms)\n        merged_vocabulary.update(vocabulary)\n\n    return merged_doc_term_freqs, merged_doc_terms, merged_vocabulary\n\n\ndef _create_chunks(documents, chunk_size):\n    \"\"\"\n    Split documents into chunks.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of documents\n        chunk_size: Size of each chunk\n\n    Returns:\n        List of document chunks\n    \"\"\"\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(documents[i : i + chunk_size])\n    return chunks\n\n\ndef _compute_tfidf_for_docs(args):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute TF-IDF vectors and norms for a chunk of documents.\n    This combines inverted index building and doc vector computation.\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_chunk_data, idf = args\n\n    # doc_chunk_data is list of (doc_id, tf_dict) tuples\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n    inverted_index_contributions = defaultdict(list)\n\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_chunk_data:\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n            # Contribute to inverted index\n            inverted_index_contributions[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return doc_vectors, doc_norms, inverted_index_contributions\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents, num_workers=None, chunk_size=500):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Steps:\n    1. Split documents into chunks\n    2. Process chunks in parallel (tokenize, compute TF)\n    3. Merge results from all chunks\n    4. Calculate document frequencies (sequential)\n    5. Compute IDF scores (sequential)\n    6. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores (parallel)\n    7. Compute document vector norms (parallel)\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Size of each chunk for processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # Determine number of workers\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Create larger chunks to reduce overhead\n    # Use fewer chunks (num_workers * 2) for better performance\n    optimal_chunk_size = max(chunk_size, len(documents) // (num_workers * 2))\n    chunks = _create_chunks(documents, optimal_chunk_size)\n\n    # Single Pool for all parallel operations to reduce overhead\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        # Step 2: Process chunks in parallel (tokenize and compute TF)\n        chunk_results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks, chunksize=1)\n\n        # Step 3: Merge results from all chunks\n        doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary = _merge_chunk_results(chunk_results)\n        index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n        # Step 4: Calculate document frequencies (sequential - fast operation)\n        for term in index.vocabulary:\n            df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n            index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n        # Step 5: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast operation)\n        N = index.num_documents\n        for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n            index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n        # Step 6 & 7: Build TF-IDF vectors, norms, and inverted index (parallel)\n        # Prepare document chunks with their TF data\n        doc_items = list(doc_term_freqs.items())\n        doc_chunks = _create_chunks(doc_items, optimal_chunk_size)\n        tfidf_args = [(chunk, index.idf) for chunk in doc_chunks]\n\n        tfidf_results = pool.map(_compute_tfidf_for_docs, tfidf_args, chunksize=1)\n\n    # Merge results\n    for doc_vectors, doc_norms, inverted_contributions in tfidf_results:\n        index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors)\n        index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms)\n\n        # Merge inverted index contributions\n        for term, postings in inverted_contributions.items():\n            if term not in index.inverted_index:\n                index.inverted_index[term] = []\n            index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n\n    # Sort inverted index posting lists by score\n    for term in index.inverted_index:\n        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n# Global variables for worker processes (set via initializer)\n_worker_index = None\n_worker_doc_titles = None\n\n\ndef _init_search_worker(index, doc_titles):\n    \"\"\"Initialize worker process with shared index and document titles.\"\"\"\n    global _worker_index, _worker_doc_titles\n    _worker_index = index\n    _worker_doc_titles = doc_titles\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(args):\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to search for a single query using pre-loaded index.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, top_k)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, top_k = args\n\n    # Use global index (set via initializer)\n    index = _worker_index\n    doc_titles = _worker_doc_titles\n\n    # Import heapq here (each worker needs it)\n    from heapq import nlargest\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(\n            SearchResult(\n                doc_id=doc_id,\n                score=score,\n                title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n            )\n        )\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(queries, index, top_k=10, num_workers=None, documents=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (List of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # Determine number of workers\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    # Prepare document titles dictionary\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Prepare arguments for each query (now just query and top_k)\n    args_list = [(query, top_k) for query in queries]\n\n    # Process queries in parallel with index pre-loaded in each worker\n    # Use smaller chunksize for better load balancing across queries\n    optimal_chunksize = max(1, len(queries) // (num_workers * 4))\n    with Pool(\n        processes=num_workers,\n        initializer=_init_search_worker,\n        initargs=(index, doc_titles),\n    ) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_search_single_query, args_list, chunksize=optimal_chunksize)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point (for testing)\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    import argparse\n\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus, load_corpus\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-docs\",\n        type=int,\n        default=5000,\n        help=\"Number of documents to generate if no corpus provided\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-queries\", type=int, default=100, help=\"Number of queries for batch search\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results to return\")\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-workers\",\n        type=int,\n        default=None,\n        help=\"Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--chunk-size\", type=int, default=500, help=\"Chunk size for parallel processing\"\n    )\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load or generate corpus\n    if args.corpus:\n        print(f\"\\nLoading corpus from {args.corpus}...\")\n        documents = load_corpus(args.corpus)\n    else:\n        print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n        documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n\n    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n    total_words = sum(d.word_count for d in documents)\n    print(f\"Total words: {total_words:,}\")\n\n    # Determine workers\n    num_workers = args.num_workers if args.num_workers else cpu_count()\n    print(f\"Using {num_workers} worker processes\")\n\n    # Build index in parallel\n    print(f\"\\nBuilding TF-IDF index in parallel (chunk_size={args.chunk_size})...\")\n    result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n        documents, num_workers=num_workers, chunk_size=args.chunk_size\n    )\n\n    print(f\"\\nIndex built in {result.elapsed_time:.3f} seconds\")\n    print(f\"Vocabulary size: {result.vocabulary_size:,} terms\")\n    print(f\"Documents indexed: {result.num_documents}\")\n\n    # Generate test queries\n    print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_queries} test queries...\")\n    import random\n\n    random.seed(42)\n    query_templates = [\n        \"machine learning algorithm\",\n        \"data processing system\",\n        \"network security protocol\",\n        \"database optimization technique\",\n        \"software development framework\",\n        \"cloud computing infrastructure\",\n        \"artificial intelligence model\",\n        \"distributed system architecture\",\n        \"medical diagnosis treatment\",\n        \"business strategy analysis\",\n    ]\n    queries = [random.choice(query_templates) for _ in range(args.num_queries)]\n\n    # Perform batch search in parallel\n    print(f\"\\nPerforming batch search ({args.num_queries} queries in parallel)...\")\n    results, search_time = batch_search_parallel(\n        queries, result.index, args.top_k, num_workers, documents\n    )\n\n    print(f\"Batch search completed in {search_time:.3f} seconds\")\n    print(f\"Average time per query: {search_time / len(queries) * 1000:.2f} ms\")\n\n    # [{res.score:.4f}] {res.title}\")\n\n    return result\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":169,"oldLines":36,"newStart":169,"newLines":37,"lines":["     index = TFIDFIndex()","     index.num_documents = len(documents)"," ","-    # Step 1: Create larger chunks to reduce overhead","-    # Use fewer chunks (num_workers * 2) for better performance","-    optimal_chunk_size = max(chunk_size, len(documents) // (num_workers * 2))","+    # Step 1: Create chunks -
